"value": "\\(\\frac{1}2\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Identify complementary angles: 68° + 22° = 90° and 17° + 73° = 90°.

\n

Using sin2θ + sin2(90°−θ) = 1, the numerator becomes 1.

\n

Using cos2θ + cos2(90°−θ) = 1, the denominator becomes 2(1) = 2.

\n

Result = 1 ÷ 2 = 1/2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1/2.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: \\(\\frac{\\sin^2 68^\\circ + \\sin^2 22^\\circ}{2(\\cos^2 17^\\circ + \\cos^2 73^\\circ)}\\)

\n

Formula Used: sin(90° − θ) = cos θ and cos(90° − θ) = sin θ; sin2θ + cos2θ = 1.

\n

⇒ Numerator: sin2 68° + sin2 (90° − 68°)

\n

⇒ Numerator: sin2 68° + cos2 68° = 1

\n

⇒ Denominator: 2 × [cos2 17° + cos2 (90° − 17°)]

\n

⇒ Denominator: 2 × [cos2 17° + sin2 17°]

\n

⇒ Denominator: 2 × 1 = 2

\n

⇒ Value = 1/2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1/2.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Complementary Angle Identities

\n

For any angle θ, sin(90° − θ) = cos θ, cos(90° − θ) = sin θ, and tan(90° − θ) = cot θ.

\n

Pythagorean Identities

\n

The most fundamental identity is sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, which applies for all real values of θ.

\n

Square Sum Shortcut

\n

If A + B = 90°, then sin2A + sin2B = 1 and cos2A + cos2B = 1. Shortcut Trick\n

पूरक कोणों (complementary angles) की पहचान करें: 68° + 22° = 90° और 17° + 73° = 90°.

\n

sin2θ + sin2(90°−θ) = 1 का उपयोग करने पर, अंश (numerator) 1 हो जाता है।

\n

cos2θ + cos2(90°−θ) = 1 का उपयोग करने पर, हर (denominator) 2(1) = 2 हो जाता है।

\n

परिणाम = 1 ÷ 2 = 1/2.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 1/2 है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है: \\(\\frac{\\sin^2 68^\\circ + \\sin^2 22^\\circ}{2(\\cos^2 17^\\circ + \\cos^2 73^\\circ)}\\)

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र: sin(90° − θ) = cos θ और cos(90° − θ) = sin θ; sin2θ + cos2θ = 1.

\n

⇒ अंश: sin2 68° + sin2 (90° − 68°)

\n

⇒ अंश: sin2 68° + cos2 68° = 1

\n

⇒ हर: 2 × [cos2 17° + cos2 (90° − 17°)]

\n

⇒ हर: 2 × [cos2 17° + sin2 17°]

\n

⇒ हर: 2 × 1 = 2

\n

⇒ मान = 1/2

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 1/2 है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

पूरक कोण सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

किसी भी कोण θ के लिए, sin(90° − θ) = cos θ, cos(90° − θ) = sin θ, और tan(90° − θ) = cot θ.

\n

पायथागोरस सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

सबसे मौलिक सर्वसमिका sin2θ + cos2θ = 1 है, जो θ के सभी वास्तविक मानों के लिए लागू होती है।

\n

वर्ग योग शॉर्टकट

\n

यदि A + B = 90°, तो sin2A + sin2B = 1 और cos2A + cos2B = 1.

ఇచ్చినవి:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\sin^2 68^\\circ + \\sin^2 22^\\circ}{2(\\cos^2 17^\\circ + \\cos^2 73^\\circ)}.\\) విలువ

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\sin^2 68^\\circ + \\sin^2 22^\\circ}{2(\\cos^2 17^\\circ + \\cos^2 73^\\circ)}.\\)

\n\n

⇒ sin2 68° + sin2 22° = (1 - cos2 68°) + (1 - cos2 22°)

\n\n

⇒ = 1 - cos2 68° + 1 - cos2 22°

\n\n

⇒ = 2 - (cos2 68° + cos2 22°)

\n\n

ఇప్పుడు, 2(cos2 17° + cos2 73°)

\n\n

⇒ cos2 17° + cos2 73° = 2[(1 - sin2 17°) + (1 - sin2 73°)]

\n\n

⇒ = 2[1 - sin2 17° + 1 - sin2 73°]

\n\n

⇒ = 4 - 2(sin2 17° + sin2 73°)

\n\n

ఈ విలువలను సమీకరణంలో తిరిగి ప్రతిక్షేపించండి:

\n\n

⇒ (2 - (cos2 68° + cos2 22°)) / (4 - 2(sin2 17° + sin2 73°)))

\n\n

⇒ (2 - 1) / (4 - 2)

\n\n

⇒ 1 / 2

\n\n

∴ సరైన సమాధానం ఎంపిక (4).

\n7d:Tb66,\n Shortcut Trick\n

From given data, P = 7X and P = Q + 70 ⇒ 7X = Q + 70.

\n

Since Q = Y + 6, substitute: 7X = (Y + 6) + 70 = Y + 76.

\n

Given equation: 7X = 2Y − 6.

\n

Equating both: Y + 76 = 2Y − 6 ⇒ Y = 82.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 82 years.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Let the present ages of X, Y, P, and Q be X, Y, P, and Q years respectively.

\n

Given Equations:

\n

1) 7X = 2Y − 6

\n

2) P = 7X

\n

3) Y = Q − 6 ⇒ Q = Y + 6

\n

4) P = Q + 70

\n

Substitute (2) and (3) into (4):

\n

⇒ 7X = (Y + 6) + 70

\n

⇒ 7X = Y + 76

\n

Now equate this with Equation (1):

\n

⇒ 2Y − 6 = Y + 76

\n

⇒ 2Y − Y = 76 + 6

\n

⇒ Y = 82

\n

∴ The correct answer is 82 years.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Linear Equations in Ages

\n

Age problems often involve translating verbal phrases like 'n years older than' or 'n times as old as' into linear algebraic equations.

\n

Substitution Method

\n

Express one variable in terms of another from one equation and substitute it into the other to solve for a single variable.

\n

Ratio and Proportion in Ages

\n

If the ratio of ages is given as a:b, we assume ages as ax and bx to simplify the calculation using a single constant x. Shortcut Trick\n

दिए गए डेटा से, P = 7X और P = Q + 70 ⇒ 7X = Q + 70.

\n

चूँकि Q = Y + 6, प्रतिस्थापित करें: 7X = (Y + 6) + 70 = Y + 76.

\n

दिया गया समीकरण: 7X = 2Y − 6.

\n

दोनों की तुलना करने पर: Y + 76 = 2Y − 6 ⇒ Y = 82.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 82 वर्ष है।

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n
ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

X యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సుకి 7 రెట్లు = (Y ప్రస్తుత వయస్సుకి 2 రెట్లు) - 6

\n\n

P యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = 7 x X యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు

\n\n

Y యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = Q యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు - 6

\n\n

P యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = Q యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు + 70

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

ఒక వేరియబుల్, Y తో ఒకే సమీకరణాన్ని రూపొందించడానికి మేము ప్రత్యామ్నాయ పద్ధతిని ఉపయోగిస్తాము.

\n\n

సాధన:

\n\n

Y కోసం ఒక సమీకరణాన్ని కనుగొనడానికి ఇచ్చిన సంబంధాలను ఉపయోగిద్దాం.

\n\n

మనకు తెలిసినది:
P = Q + 70

\n\n

P = 7X మరియు Q = Y + 6 అని కూడా మనకు తెలుసు. ఈ విలువలను మొదటి సమీకరణంలో ప్రతిక్షేపించుదాం.

\n\n

⇒ 7X = (Y + 6) + 70

\n\n

⇒ 7X = Y + 76

\n\n

X మరియు Y మధ్య మరొక సంబంధం ఇవ్వబడింది:

\n\n

⇒ 7X = 2Y - 6

\n\n

ఇప్పుడు, 7X కోసం రెండు వ్యక్తీకరణలను సమానం చేయవచ్చు:

\n\n

⇒ Y + 76 = 2Y - 6

\n\n

⇒ 76 + 6 = 2Y - Y

\n\n

⇒ 82 = Y

\n\n

∴ Y యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు 82 సంవత్సరాలు.

दिलेले आहे:

\n\n

X च्या सध्याच्या वयाच्या 7 पट = (Y च्या सध्याच्या वयाच्या 2 पट) - 6

\n\n

P चे सध्याचे वय = 7 × X चे सध्याचे वय

\n\n

Y चे सध्याचे वय = Q चे सध्याचे वय - 6

\n\n

P चे सध्याचे वय = Q चे सध्याचे वय + 70

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

आपण केवळ Y, हे एक चल असलेल्या समीकरणाची निर्मिती करण्यासाठी प्रतिस्थापन पद्धत वापरणार आहोत.

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

Y साठीचे समीकरण शोधण्यासाठी, दिलेल्या संबंधांचा वापर करूयात.

\n\n

आपल्याला माहित आहे की:
\nP = Q + 70

\n\n

आपल्याला हे देखील माहित आहे की, P = 7X आणि Q = Y + 6.
\nही मूल्ये पहिल्या समीकरणात ठेवू.

\n\n

⇒ 7X = (Y + 6) + 70

\n\n

⇒ 7X = Y + 76

\n\n

आपल्याला X आणि Y यांच्यातील आणखी एक संबंध दिला आहे:

\n\n

⇒ 7X = 2Y - 6

\n\n

आता, आपण 7X साठी दोन पदावली समान करू शकतो:

\n\n

⇒ Y + 76 = 2Y - 6

\n\n

\n\n

\nP = Q + 70

\n\n

⇒ 82 = Y

P ର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସ = Q ର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସ + 70

\n\n

ସୂତ୍ର ବ୍ୟବହାର କରାଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ଆମେ କେବଳ ଗୋଟିଏ ଚଳରାଶି, Y ସହିତ ଏକ ସମୀକରଣ ଗଠନ କରିବା ପାଇଁ ଏକ ସବଷ୍ଟିଟ୍ୟୁସନ୍ ପଦ୍ଧତି ବ୍ୟବହାର କରିବୁ |

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

Y ପାଇଁ ଏକ ସମୀକରଣ ଖୋଜିବା ପାଇଁ ଦିଆଯାଇଥିବା ସମ୍ପର୍କଗୁଡ଼ିକୁ ବ୍ୟବହାର କରିବା |

\n\n

ଆମେ ଜାଣୁ ଯେ:
\nP = Q + 70

\n\n

ଆମେ ଏହା ମଧ୍ୟ ଜାଣୁ ଯେ P = 7X ଏବଂ Q = Y + 6 | ଆସନ୍ତୁ ଏହି ମୂଲ୍ୟଗୁଡ଼ିକୁ ପ୍ରଥମ ସମୀକରଣରେ ବଦଳାଇବା |

\n\n

⇒ 7X = (Y + 6) + 70

\n\n

⇒ 7X = Y + 76

\n\n

ଆମକୁ X ଏବଂ Y ମଧ୍ୟରେ ଅନ୍ୟ ଏକ ସମ୍ପର୍କ ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

⇒ 7X = 2Y - 6

\n\n

ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ, ଆମେ 7X ପାଇଁ ଦୁଇଟି ଅଭିବ୍ୟକ୍ତିକୁ ସମାନ କରିପାରିବା:

\n\n

⇒ Y + 76 = 2Y - 6

\n\n

⇒ 76 + 6 = 2Y - Y

\n\n

⇒ 82 = Y

\n\n

∴ Y ର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସ ହେଉଛି 82 ବର୍ଷ |

"\\(\\frac{50}{\\sqrt{2}} \\) ਮੀਟਰ"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

In a 30°−60°−90° triangle, the ratio of sides opposite to these angles is 1 : √3 : 2.

\n

The side opposite to 30° (height) is 1 unit = 50 m.

\n

The side opposite to 60° (distance from foot) is √3 units.

\n

Distance = 50 × √3 = 50√3 m.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 50√3 m.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given:

\n

Height of the tower (QR) = 50 m

\n

Angle of elevation at point P (θ) = 30°

\n

Formula Used:

\n

tan θ = Perpendicular / Base

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ tan 30° = QR / PQ

\n

⇒ 1 / √3 = 50 / PQ

\n

⇒ PQ = 50 × √3

\n

⇒ PQ = 50√3 m

\n

∴ The correct answer is 50√3 m.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Ratios

\n

Common values: tan 30° = 1/√3, tan 45° = 1, tan 60° = √3.

\n

Angle of Elevation vs Depression

\n

The angle of elevation from an observer to an object is equal to the angle of depression from the object to the observer due to alternate interior angles.

\n

Standard Triangle Ratios

\n

For a 45°−45°−90° triangle, the side ratio is 1 : 1 : √2. For a 30°−60°−90° triangle, it is 1 : √3 : 2. Shortcut Trick\n

एक 30°−60°−90° त्रिभुज में, इन कोणों के सम्मुख भुजाओं का अनुपात 1 : √3 : 2 होता है।

\n

30° के सम्मुख भुजा (ऊंचाई) 1 इकाई = 50 m है।

\n

60° के सम्मुख भुजा (पाद से दूरी) √3 इकाई है।

\n

दूरी = 50 × √3 = 50√3 m.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 50√3 m है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

दिया गया है:

\n

टावर की ऊंचाई (QR) = 50 m

\n

बिंदु P पर उन्नयन कोण (θ) = 30°

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n

tan θ = लंब / आधार

\n

गणना:

\n

⇒ tan 30° = QR / PQ

\n

⇒ 1 / √3 = 50 / PQ

\n

⇒ PQ = 50 × √3

\n

⇒ PQ = 50√3 m

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 50√3 m है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

त्रिकोणमितीय अनुपात

\n

सामान्य मान: tan 30° = 1/√3, tan 45° = 1, tan 60° = √3.

\n

उन्नयन कोण बनाम अवनमन कोण

\n

एकांतर अंतः कोणों के कारण एक पर्यवेक्षक से वस्तु का उन्नयन कोण, वस्तु से पर्यवेक्षक के अवनमन कोण के बराबर होता है।

\n

मानक त्रिभुज अनुपात

\n

45°−45°−90° त्रिभुज के लिए, भुजाओं का अनुपात 1 : 1 : √2 होता है। 30°−60°−90° त्रिभुज के लिए, यह 1 : √3 : 2 होता है।

\n7f:Tc1d,\n Shortcut Trick\n

From given data, P = 7X and P = Q + 70 ⇒ 7X = Q + 70.

\n

Since Q = Y + 6, substitute: 7X = (Y + 6) + 70 = Y + 76.

\n

Given equation: 7X = 2Y − 6.

\n

Equating both: Y + 76 = 2Y − 6 ⇒ Y = 82.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 82 years.

\n\n

ఇచ్చినవి:

\n\n

టవర్ ఎత్తు = 50 మీ

\n\n

ఊర్థ్వ కోణం = 30°

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

లంబ కోణ త్రిభుజంలో, tan(θ) = ఎదుటి భుజం ÷ ఆసన్న భుజం

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

P బిందువు నుండి టవర్ పాదం వరకు ఉన్న దూరం 'd' అనుకుందాం. టవర్ ఎత్తు ఎదుటి భుజం మరియు 'd' ఆసన్న భుజం.

\n\n

⇒ tan(30°) = 50 ÷ d

\n\n

⇒ 1 ÷ √3 = 50 ÷ d

\n\n

⇒ d = 50 x √3

\n\n

⇒ d = 50√3 మీ

\n\n

∴ టవర్ పాదం నుండి P బిందువు యొక్క దూరం 50√3 మీ.

दिलेले आहे:

\n\n

मनोऱ्याची उंची = 50 मीटर

\n\n

उन्नत कोन = 30°

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

काटकोन त्रिकोणामध्ये, tan(θ) = समोरची बाजू ÷ लगतची बाजू

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

समजा, बिंदू P पासून मनोऱ्याच्या पायापर्यंतचे अंतर 'd' आहे. मनोऱ्याची उंची ही समोरची बाजू आहे आणि 'd' ही लगतची बाजू आहे.

\n\n

⇒ tan(30°) = 50 ÷ d

\n\n

⇒ 1 ÷ √3 = 50 ÷ d

\n\n

⇒ d = 50 × √3

\n\n

⇒ d = 50√3 मीटर

\n\n

∴ बिंदू P चे मनोऱ्याच्या पायापासूनचे अंतर 50√3 मीटर आहे.

\n\n

\n\n

একটি সমকোণী ত্রিভুজে, tan(θ) = বিপরীত বাহু ÷ সংলগ্ন বাহু

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

ধরা যাক, P বিন্দু থেকে টাওয়ারের পাদদেশের দূরত্ব 'd'। টাওয়ারের উচ্চতা হল বিপরীত বাহু এবং 'd' হল সংলগ্ন বাহু।

\n\n

⇒ tan(30°) = 50 ÷ d

\n\n

⇒ 1 ÷ √3 = 50 ÷ d

\n\n

⇒ d = 50 x √3

\n\n

⇒ d = 50√3 মি

\n\n

∴ টাওয়ারের পাদদেশ থেকে P বিন্দুর দূরত্ব হল 50√3 মি।

\n", "ml": "", "gu": "

ਉਚਾਈ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ।", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "29.6 ਮੀਟਰ"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "27.6 ਮੀਟਰ"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "28.6 ਮੀਟਰ"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "26.6 ਮੀਟਰ"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

When the angle of elevation is 45°, the height of the tower and its base distance (shadow) are always equal because tan 45° = 1.

\n

Ratio of Height : Base = 1 : 1

\n

Since the base distance is 27.6 m, the height must also be 27.6 m.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 27.6 m.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given:

\n

Distance from foot (Base) = 27.6 m

\n

Angle of elevation (θ) = 45°

\n

Formula Used:

\n

tan θ = Perpendicular (Height) ÷ Base

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ tan 45° = Height ÷ 27.6

\n

⇒ 1 = Height ÷ 27.6

\n

⇒ Height = 27.6 × 1

\n

⇒ Height = 27.6 m

\n

∴ The correct answer is 27.6 m.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Ratios for Standard Angles

\n

tan 30° = 1/√3, tan 45° = 1, and tan 60° = √3. These are frequently used in height and distance problems.

\n

Special Right Triangle (45-45-90)

\n

In a 45°−45°−90° triangle, the legs are equal in length, and the hypotenuse is leg × √2.

\n

Angle of Elevation vs Depression

\n

The angle of elevation from an observer's eye to an object is numerically equal to the angle of depression from the object to the observer's eye due to alternate interior angles. Shortcut Trick\n

जब उन्नयन कोण (angle of elevation) 45° होता है, तो मीनार की ऊंचाई और उसकी आधार दूरी (छाया) हमेशा बराबर होती है क्योंकि tan 45° = 1 होता है।

\n

ऊंचाई : आधार का अनुपात = 1 : 1

\n

चूँकि आधार की दूरी 27.6 मीटर है, इसलिए ऊंचाई भी 27.6 मीटर होनी चाहिए।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 27.6 मीटर है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

दिया गया है:

\n

पाद से दूरी (आधार) = 27.6 मीटर

\n

उन्नयन कोण (θ) = 45°

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n

tan θ = लंब (ऊंचाई) ÷ आधार

\n

गणना:

\n

⇒ tan 45° = ऊंचाई ÷ 27.6

\n

⇒ 1 = ऊंचाई ÷ 27.6

\n

⇒ ऊंचाई = 27.6 × 1

\n

⇒ ऊंचाई = 27.6 मीटर

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 27.6 मीटर है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

मानक कोणों के लिए त्रिकोणमितीय अनुपात

\n

tan 30° = 1/√3, tan 45° = 1, और tan 60° = √3। इनका उपयोग अक्सर ऊंचाई और दूरी की समस्याओं में किया जाता है।

\n

विशेष समकोण त्रिभुज (45-45-90)

\n

एक 45°−45°−90° त्रिभुज में, भुजाओं की लंबाई बराबर होती है, और कर्ण = भुजा × √2 होता है।

\n

उन्नयन कोण बनाम अवनमन कोण

\n

किसी प्रेक्षक की आंख से किसी वस्तु का उन्नयन कोण, एकांतर आंतरिक कोणों के कारण वस्तु से प्रेक्षक की आंख के अवनमन कोण के संख्यात्मक रूप से बराबर होता है।

\n86:Tc03,\n Shortcut Trick\n

Given secθ + tanθ = 2. Then secθ − tanθ = 1/2.

\n

Adding both: 2secθ = 2 + 1/2 = 5/2 ⇒ secθ = 5/4.

\n

Find: 3secθ + 4 = 3(5/4) + 4 = 15/4 + 16/4 = 31/4.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 31/4.

\n

\n\n

\n\n

ఊర్థ్వ కోణం = 45º

\n\n

{"value": "ଏକ କାନ୍ଥରେ ଆଉଜି ରହିଥିବା ଏକ ସିଡ଼ିର ଉନ୍ନତି କୋଣ 60° ଏବଂ ସିଡ଼ିର ପାଦ କାନ୍ଥଠାରୁ 4.5 ମିଟର ଦୂରରେ ଅଛି | ସିଡ଼ିର ଦୈର୍ଘ୍ୟ କେତେ?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "\\(9 m \\\\\\)"}, {"prompt": "2", ਪੌੜੀ ਦਾ ਉਚਾਈ ਕੋਣ 60° ਹੈ, ਅਤੇ ਪੌੜੀ ਦਾ ਪੈਰ ਕੰਧ ਤੋਂ 4.5 ਮੀਟਰ ਦੂਰ ਹੈ। ਪੌੜੀ ਦੀ ਲੰਬਾਈ ਕਿੰਨੀ ਹੈ?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "\\(9 m \\\\\\)"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "\\(8 m \\\\\\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(3\\sqrt{2} m \\\\\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(4\\sqrt{2} m\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick

In a 30°-60°-90° triangle, the ratio of sides is 1 : √3 : 2.

Side opposite to 30° (base) = 1 unit = 4.5 m.

The ladder is the hypotenuse (opposite to 90°), which is 2 units.

Length of ladder = 2 × 4.5 = 9 m.

∴ The correct answer is 9 m.

Alternate Method

Given:

Angle of elevation (θ) = 60°

Distance from wall (Base) = 4.5 m

Formula Used:

cos θ = Base / Hypotenuse

Calculations:

⇒ cos 60° = 4.5 / Length of ladder

⇒ 1/2 = 4.5 / Length of ladder

⇒ Length of ladder = 4.5 × 2

⇒ Length of ladder = 9 m

∴ The correct answer is 9 m.

Additional Information

Trigonometric Ratios

For a right-angled triangle, sin θ = P/H, cos θ = B/H, and tan θ = P/B where P is perpendicular, B is base, and H is hypotenuse.

Standard Values

Commonly used values: sin 30° = 1/2, cos 60° = 1/2, tan 45° = 1, and tan 60° = √3.

Angle of Elevation

The angle formed between the horizontal line and the line of sight when looking up at an object. Shortcut Trick

एक 30°-60°-90° त्रिभुज में, भुजाओं का अनुपात 1 : √3 : 2 होता है।

30° के सम्मुख भुजा (आधार) = 1 यूनिट = 4.5 m है।

सीढ़ी कर्ण (90° के सम्मुख) है, जो 2 यूनिट है।

सीढ़ी की लंबाई = 2 × 4.5 = 9 m है।

∴ सही उत्तर 9 m है।

Alternate Method

दिया गया है:

उन्नयन कोण (θ) = 60°

दीवार से दूरी (आधार) = 4.5 m

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

cos θ = आधार / कर्ण

गणना:

⇒ cos 60° = 4.5 / सीढ़ी की लंबाई

⇒ 1/2 = 4.5 / सीढ़ी की लंबाई

⇒ सीढ़ी की लंबाई = 4.5 × 2

⇒ सीढ़ी की लंबाई = 9 m

∴ सही उत्तर 9 m है।

Additional Information

त्रिकोणमितीय अनुपात

एक समकोण त्रिभुज के लिए, sin θ = P/H, cos θ = B/H, और tan θ = P/B जहाँ P लंब है, B आधार है, और H कर्ण है।

मानक मान

आमतौर पर उपयोग किए जाने वाले मान: sin 30° = 1/2, cos 60° = 1/2, tan 45° = 1, और tan 60° = √3।

उन्नयन कोण

Apply Componendo and Dividendo directly: (secθ − tanθ + secθ + tanθ) / (secθ − tanθ − secθ − tanθ) = (3 + 5) / (3 − 5).

\n

⇒ 2secθ / (−2tanθ) = 8 / (−2)

\n

⇒ secθ / tanθ = 4

\n

⇒ (1/cosθ) / (sinθ/cosθ) = 4 ⇒ 1 / sinθ = 4

\n

⇒ sinθ = 1/4

\n

⇒ 2sinθ = 2 × (1/4) = 1/2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1/2.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given: (secθ − tanθ) / (secθ + tanθ) = 3 / 5

\n

Cross Multiplying:

\n

⇒ 5(secθ − tanθ) = 3(secθ + tanθ)

\n

⇒ 5secθ − 5tanθ = 3secθ + 3tanθ

\n

⇒ 5secθ − 3secθ = 3tanθ + 5tanθ

\n

⇒ 2secθ = 8tanθ

\n

⇒ secθ / tanθ = 8 / 2 = 4

\n

⇒ (1 / cosθ) × (cosθ / sinθ) = 4

\n

⇒ 1 / sinθ = 4

\n

⇒ sinθ = 1 / 4

\n

⇒ 2sinθ = 2 × (1/4) = 1/2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1/2.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Componendo and Dividendo Rule

\n

If a/b = c/d, then (a + b) / (a − b) = (c + d) / (c − d). This is very useful in simplifying trigonometric ratios.

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Key identities: sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, hence (secθ − tanθ) = 1 / (secθ + tanθ).

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

secθ = 1/cosθ, cosecθ = 1/sinθ, and tanθ = sinθ/cosθ. Shortcut Trick\n

सीधे Componendo and Dividendo लागू करें: (secθ − tanθ + secθ + tanθ) / (secθ − tanθ − secθ − tanθ) = (3 + 5) / (3 − 5)।

\n

⇒ 2secθ / (−2tanθ) = 8 / (−2)

\n

⇒ secθ / tanθ = 4

\n

⇒ (1/cosθ) / (sinθ/cosθ) = 4 ⇒ 1 / sinθ = 4

\n

⇒ sinθ = 1/4

\n

⇒ 2sinθ = 2 × (1/4) = 1/2

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 1/2 है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

दिया गया है: (secθ − tanθ) / (secθ + tanθ) = 3 / 5

\n

तिर्यक गुणा (Cross Multiplying) करने पर:

\n

⇒ 5(secθ − tanθ) = 3(secθ + tanθ)

\n

⇒ 5secθ − 5tanθ = 3secθ + 3tanθ

\n

⇒ 5secθ − 3secθ = 3tanθ + 5tanθ

\n

⇒ 2secθ = 8tanθ

\n

⇒ secθ / tanθ = 8 / 2 = 4

\n

⇒ (1 / cosθ) × (cosθ / sinθ) = 4

\n

⇒ 1 / sinθ = 4

\n

⇒ sinθ = 1 / 4

\n

⇒ 2sinθ = 2 × (1/4) = 1/2

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 1/2 है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

Componendo and Dividendo नियम

\n

यदि a/b = c/d, तो (a + b) / (a − b) = (c + d) / (c − d) है। यह त्रिकोणमितीय अनुपातों को सरल बनाने में बहुत उपयोगी है।

\n

त्रिकोणमितीय सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

मुख्य सर्वसमिकाएँ: sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, इसलिए (secθ − tanθ) = 1 / (secθ + tanθ)।

\n

व्युत्क्रम संबंध

\n

secθ = 1/cosθ, cosecθ = 1/sinθ, और tanθ = sinθ/cosθ।

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ \\((5 - 3 = 5\\sinθ + 3\\sinθ)\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((2 = 8\\sinθ)\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\sinθ = \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((2\\sinθ = 2 \\times \\dfrac{1}{4})\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(2\\sinθ = \\dfrac{1}{2}\\)

\n\n

∴ సరైన సమాధానం ఎంపిక (2).

\\(\\dfrac{(\\secθ - \\tanθ)}{(\\secθ + \\tanθ)} = \\dfrac{3}{5}\\)

\n\n

ব্যবহৃত সূত্র:

\n\n

\\(\\secθ = \\dfrac{1}{\\cosθ}\\) এবং \\(\\tanθ = \\dfrac{\\sinθ}{\\cosθ}\\)

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{(\\secθ - \\tanθ)}{(\\secθ + \\tanθ)} = \\dfrac{3}{5}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{(\\dfrac{1}{\\cosθ} - \\dfrac{\\sinθ}{\\cosθ})}{(\\dfrac{1}{\\cosθ} + \\dfrac{\\sinθ}{\\cosθ})} = \\dfrac{3}{5}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{(1 - \\sinθ)}{(1 + \\sinθ)} = \\dfrac{3}{5}\\)

\n\n

⇒ বজ্রগুণন: \\((5(1 - \\sinθ) = 3(1 + \\sinθ))\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((5 - 5\\sinθ = 3 + 3\\sinθ)\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((5 - 3 = 5\\sinθ + 3\\sinθ)\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((2 = 8\\sinθ)\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\sinθ = \\dfrac{1}{4}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((2\\sinθ = 2 \\times \\dfrac{1}{4})\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(2\\sinθ = \\dfrac{1}{2}\\)

\n\n

∴ সঠিক উত্তর হল বিকল্প (2).

Let P and Q be the present ages.

\n

Equation 1: 4P − Q = 35 ⇒ Q = 4P − 35

\n

Equation 2: 2(Q + 8) = 6(P + 8) − 40 ⇒ 2Q + 16 = 6P + 48 − 40 ⇒ 2Q = 6P − 8 ⇒ Q = 3P − 4

\n

Equating Q: 4P − 35 = 3P − 4 ⇒ P = 31 years.

\n

Put P in Q = 3(31) − 4 = 93 − 4 = 89 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 89 years.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given:

\n

⇒ 4 × P = Q + 35 —— (i)

\n

⇒ 2 × (Q + 8) = 6 × (P + 8) − 40 —— (ii)

\n

From (i): Q = 4P − 35

\n

Substitute Q in (ii):

\n

⇒ 2(4P − 35 + 8) = 6P + 48 − 40

\n

⇒ 2(4P − 27) = 6P + 8

\n

⇒ 8P − 54 = 6P + 8

\n

⇒ 2P = 62

\n

⇒ P = 31 years

\n

Now, find Q:

\n

⇒ Q = 4(31) − 35 = 124 − 35 = 89 years

\n

∴ The correct answer is 89 years.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Linear Equations in Ages

\n

Problems involving two variables are solved by forming two simultaneous equations based on past, present, or future conditions.

\n

Age Ratio Constant

\n

The difference between ages of two people remains constant regardless of the number of years passed or preceding.

\n

Future and Past Ages

\n

If present age is x, age after 'n' years is (x + n) and age 'n' years ago was (x − n).दिया गया है:

\n\n

P की वर्तमान आयु का 4 गुना, Q की वर्तमान आयु से 35 वर्ष अधिक है।

\n\n

8 वर्ष बाद, Q की आयु का 2 गुना, P की आयु के 6 गुने से 40 वर्ष कम होगा।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए P की वर्तमान आयु = x वर्ष और Q की वर्तमान आयु = y वर्ष है।

\n\n

कथनों से:

\n\n

4x = y + 35 ⇒ y = 4x - 35 ----- (1)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 2(4ఇచ్చినవి:

\n\n

\n\n

8 సంవత్సరాల తర్వాత, Q యొక్క వయస్సుకి 2 రెట్లు P యొక్క వయస్సుకి 6 రెట్లు కంటే 40 సంవత్సరాలు తక్కువగా ఉంటుంది.

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

P యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = x సంవత్సరాలు మరియు Q యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = y సంవత్సరాలు అనుకుందాం.

\n\n

ప్రకటనల నుండి:

\n\n

4x = y + 35 ⇒ y = 4x - 35 ----- (1)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n49:Ta43,\n Shortcut Trick\n

Identify the Pythagorean triplet for sin A = 4/5 → (3, 4, 5).

\n

Here, Perpendicular (P) = 4, Hypotenuse (H) = 5, and கொடுக்கப்பட்டவை:

\n\n

P-ன் தற்போதைய வயதின் 4 மடங்கு, Q-ன் தற்போதைய வயதை விட 35 ஆண்டுகள் அதிகம்.

\n\n

8 ஆண்டுகளுக்குப் பிறகு, Q-ன் வயதின் 2 மடங்கு, P-ன் வயதின் 6 மடங்கை விட 40 ஆண்டுகள் குறைவாக இருக்கும்.

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

P-ன் தற்போதைய வயது = x ஆண்டுகள் மற்றும் Q-ன் தற்போதைய வயது = y ஆண்டுகள்.

\n\n

கூற்றுகளிலிருந்து:

\n\n

4x = y + 35 ⇒ y = 4x - 35 ----- (1)

\n\n

2(y + 8) = 6(x + 8) - 40 ------(2)

\n\n

சமன்பாடு 2 இல் y = 4x - 35 ஐப் பிரதியிடவும்:

\n\n

⇒ 2(4x - 35 + 8) = 6(x + 8) - 40

\n\n

⇒ 2(4x - 27) = 6x + 48 - 40

\n\n

⇒ 8x - 54 = 6x + 8

\n\n

⇒ 8x - 6x = 8 + 54

\n\n

⇒ 2x = 62 ⇒ x = 31

\n\n

இப்போது, y = 4x - 35 இல் x = 31 ஐப் பிரதியிடவும்:

\n\n

⇒ y = 4(31) - 35

\n\n

⇒ y = 124 - 35

\n\n

⇒ y = 89

\n\n

∴ Q-ன் தற்போதைய வயது 89 ஆண்டுகள்.

"3", "value": "\\(\\frac{208}{15}\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(\\frac{3}{46}\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Identify the Pythagorean triplet for sin A = 4/5 → (3, 4, 5).

\n

Here, Perpendicular (P) = 4, Hypotenuse (H) = 5, and Base (B) = 3.

\n

Calculate required ratios: tan A = P/B = 4/3 and cos A = B/H = 3/5.

\n

Substitute in expression: (2 + 4/3) × (4 + 3/5) = (10/3) × (23/5) = 46/3.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 46/3.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: sin A = 4/5

\n

Formula Used: sin A = P/H, cos A = B/H, tan A = P/B, where B = √(H2 − P2)

\n
\n

⇒ B = √(52 − 42) = √(25 − 16) = √9 = 3

\n

⇒ tan A = 4/3

\n

⇒ cos A = 3/5

\n

⇒ Value = (2 + tan A)(4 + cos A)

\n

⇒ Value = (2 + 4/3) × (4 + 3/5)

\n

⇒ Value = (10/3) × (23/5)

\n

⇒ Value = 230/15 = 46/3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 46/3.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Fundamental relations include sin2A + cos2A = 1 and tan A = sin A / cos A.

\n

Common Pythagorean Triplets

\n

Frequently used sets in exams: (3, 4, 5), (5, 12, 13), (7, 24, 25), and (8, 15, 17). Shortcut Trick\n

sin A = 4/5 के लिए पाइथागोरस त्रिक (Pythagorean triplet) की पहचान करें → (3, 4, 5)।

\n

यहाँ, लंब (P) = 4, कर्ण (H) = 5, और आधार (B) = 3।

\n

आवश्यक अनुपातों की गणना करें: tan A = P/B = 4/3 और cos A = B/H = 3/5।

\n

व्यंजक में मान रखें: (2 + 4/3) × (4 + 3/5) = (10/3) × (23/5) = 46/3।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 46/3 है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है: sin A = 4/5

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र: sin A = P/H, cos A = B/H, tan A = P/B, जहाँ B = √(H2 − P2)

\n
\n

⇒ B = √(52 − 42) = √(25 − 16) = √9 = 3

\n

⇒ tan A = 4/3

\n

⇒ cos A = 3/5

\n

⇒ मान = (2 + tan A)(4 + cos A)

\n

⇒ मान = (2 + 4/3) × (4 + 3/5)

\n

⇒ मान = (10/3) × (23/5)

\n

⇒ मान = 230/15 = 46/3

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 46/3 है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

त्रिकोणमितीय सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

मौलिक संबंधों में sin2A + cos2A = 1 और tan A = sin A / cos A शामिल हैं।

\n

सामान्य पाइथागोरस त्रिक

\n

परीक्षाओं में अक्सर उपयोग किए जाने वाले सेट: (3, 4, 5), (5, 12, 13), (7, 24, 25), और (8, 15, 17)।

\n4b:Tb54,\n Shortcut Trick\n

For complementary angles, if sin X = cos Y, then X + Y = 90°.

\n

Given: sin 5A = cos(A − 42°)

\n

⇒ 5A + (A − 42°) = 90°

\n

⇒ 6A = ", "te": "

{"prompt": "4", "value": "5"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Let Sujatha's age be S and Vanita's age be V.

\n

Equation 1: 5S − 2V = 5

\n

Equation 2 (after 4 years): 5(S + 4) − 3(V + 4) = 8 ⇒ 5S − 3V = 0

\n

From Eq 2: 5S = 3V. Substitute in Eq 1: 3V − 2V = 5 ⇒ V = 5.

\n

Since 5S = 3(5), S = 3. Difference k = V − S = 5 − 3 = 2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2 years.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: Relation between present ages and ages after 4 years.

\n
\n

⇒ Let Sujatha's present age = S and Vanita's present age = V.

\n

⇒ From the first condition: 5S = 2V + 5 — (i)

\n

⇒ Age after 4 years: Sujatha = S + 4, Vanita = V + 4.

\n

⇒ From the second condition: 3(V + 4) = 5(S + 4) − 8

\n

⇒ 3V + 12 = 5S + 20 − 8

\n

⇒ 3V + 12 = 5S + 12

\n

⇒ 3V = 5S — (ii)

\n

⇒ Substitute 5S = 3V from (ii) into (i):

\n

⇒ 3V = 2V + 5

\n

⇒ V = 5 years

\n

⇒ Now, 5S = 3(5) ⇒ S = 3 years

\n

⇒ Difference k = V − S = 5 − 3 = 2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2 years.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Age Difference Property

\n

The difference between the ages of two persons remains constant at any point in time.

\n

Linear Equations in Two Variables

\n

Problems involving two people and two conditions are best solved using substitution or elimination methods.

\n

Ratio of Ages

\n

If the ratio of ages is given, use a common multiplier 'x' to represent actual ages and solve the linear equation. Shortcut Trick\n

माना सुजाता की आयु S है और वनिता की आयु V है।

\n

समीकरण 1: 5S − 2V = 5

\n

समीकरण 2 (4 वर्ष बाद): 5(S + 4) − 3(V + 4) = 8 ⇒ 5S − 3V = 0

\n

समीकरण 2 से: 5S = 3V. इसे समीकरण 1 में प्रतिस्थापित करने पर: 3V − 2V = 5 ⇒ V = 5.

\n

चूँकि 5S = 3(5), S = 3. अंतर k = V − S = 5 − 3 = 2.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 2 वर्ष है।

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है: वर्तमान आयु और 4 वर्ष बाद की आयु के बीच संबंध।

\n
\n

⇒ माना सुजाता की वर्तमान आयु = S और वनिता की वर्तमान आयु = V है।

\n

⇒ पहली शर्त से: 5S = 2V + 5 — (i)

\n

⇒ 4 वर्ष बाद आयु: सुजाता = S + 4, वनिता = V + 4.

\n

⇒ दूसरी शर्त से: 3(V + 4) = 5(S + 4) − 8

\n

⇒ 3V + 12 = 5S + 20 − 8

\n

⇒ 3V + 12 = 5S + 12

\n

⇒ 3V = 5S — (ii)

\n

⇒ समीकरण (ii) से 5S = 3V को (i) में प्रतिस्थापित करने पर:

\n

⇒ 3V = 2V + 5

\n

⇒ V = 5 वर्ष

\n

⇒ अब, 5S = 3(5) ⇒ S = 3 वर्ष

\n

⇒ अंतर k = V − S = 5 − 3 = 2

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 2 वर्ष है।

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

आयु अंतर गुणधर्म (Age Difference Property)

\n

दो व्यक्तियों की आयु के बीच का अंतर समय के किसी भी बिंदु पर स्थिर (constant) रहता है।

\n

दो चरों वाले रैखिक समीकरण (Linear Equations in Two Variables)

\n

दो व्यक्तियों और दो शर्तों वाले प्रश्नों को प्रतिस्थापन (substitution) या विलोपन (elimination) विधियों का उपयोग करके सबसे अच्छी तरह हल किया जाता है।

\n

आयु का अनुपात (Ratio of Ages)

\n

यदि आयु का अनुपात दिया गया है, तो वास्तविक आयु को दर्शाने के लिए एक सामान्य गुणक 'x' का उपयोग करें और रैखिक समीकरण को हल करें।

2\\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ.\\) ର ମୂଲ୍ୟ ଖୋଜ |", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "0"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "2"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "-1"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "1"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "pa": {"value": "\\(\\frac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\frac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2\\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ.\\) ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ।", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "0"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "2"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "-1"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "1"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Identify complementary angles: (35° + 55° = 90°), (11° + 79° = 90°), and (27° + 63° = 90°).

\n

Apply the property: If A + B = 90°, then sin A / cos B = 1 and cos A / sin B = 1.

\n

Also, cos A × cosec B = 1 if A + B = 90°.

\n

Expression becomes: 1 + 1 − 2(1) = 0.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: cos 35°/sin 55° + sin 11°/cos 79° − 2 cos 27° cosec 63°

\n

Formula Used: sin(90° − θ) = cos θ | cos(90° − θ) = sin θ | cosec(90° − θ) = sec θ

\n

⇒ cos 35° = cos(90° − 55°) = sin 55°

\n

⇒ sin 11° = sin(90° − 79°) = cos 79°

\n

⇒ cosec 63° = cosec(90° − 27°) = sec 27°

\n

⇒ Expression = (sin 55° / sin 55°) + (cos 79° / cos 79°) − 2 × cos 27° × sec 27°

\n

⇒ Since cos θ × sec θ = 1, the term 2 × cos 27° × sec 27° = 2 × 1

\n

⇒ Expression = 1 + 1 − 2 = 0

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Complementary Angle Identities

\n

Trigonometric ratios of complementary angles: sin(90° − θ) = cos θ, tan(90° − θ) = cot θ, and sec(90° − θ) = cosec θ.

\n

Reciprocal Identities

\n

Standard reciprocal pairs include sin θ × cosec θ = 1, cos θ × sec θ = 1, and tan θ × cot θ = 1.

\n

Product Property for Complementary Angles

\n

If A + B = 90°, then tan A × tan B = 1 and cot A × cot B = 1. Shortcut Trick\n

पूरक कोणों (complementary angles) को पहचानें: (35° + 55° = 90°), (11° + 79° = 90°), और (27° + 63° = 90°)।

\n

गुणधर्म लागू करें: यदि A + B = 90°, तो sin A / cos B = 1 और cos A / sin B = 1 है।

\n

साथ ही, यदि A + B = 90° हो, तो cos A × cosec B = 1 होता है।

\n

व्यंजक बन जाता है: 1 + 1 − 2(1) = 0.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 0 है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है: cos 35°/sin 55° + sin 11°/cos 79° − 2 cos 27° cosec 63°

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र: sin(90° − θ) = cos θ | cos(90° − θ) = sin θ | cosec(90° − θ) = sec θ

\n

⇒ cos 35° = cos(90° − 55°) = sin 55°

\n

⇒ sin 11° = sin(90° − 79°) = cos 79°

\n

⇒ cosec 63° = cosec(90° − 27°) = sec 27°

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = (sin 55° / sin 55°) + (cos 79° / cos 79°) − 2 × cos 27° × sec 27°

\n

⇒ चूँकि cos θ × sec θ = 1, इसलिए पद 2 × cos 27° × sec 27° = 2 × 1

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = 1 + 1 − 2 = 0

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 0 है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

पूरक कोण सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

पूरक कोणों के त्रिकोणमितीय अनुपात: sin(90° − θ) = cos θ, tan(90° − θ) = cot θ, और sec(90° − θ) = cosec θ.

\n

व्युत्क्रम सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

मानक व्युत्क्रम युग्मों में sin θ × cosec θ = 1, cos θ × sec θ = 1, और tan θ × cot θ = 1 शामिल हैं।

\n

पूरक कोणों के लिए गुणनफल गुणधर्म

\n

यदि A + B = 90°, तो tan A × tan B = 1 और cot A × cot B = 1 होता है।

ఇచ్చినవి:

\n\n

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\)

\n\n

\\(\\cos (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\sin \\theta\\)

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin \\theta}\\)

\n\n

లెక్కలు:

\n\n

వ్యక్తీకరణ: \\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\) మరియు \\(\\cos (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\sin \\theta\\) ఉపయోగించి:

\n\n

\\(\\sin 55^\\circ = \\cos 35^\\circ\\) మరియు \\(\\cos 79^\\circ = \\sin 11^\\circ\\)

\n\n

వ్యక్తీకరణ అవుతుంది:

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\cos 35^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\sin 11^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((1 + 1 - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ)\\)

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin \\theta}\\) ఉపయోగించి:

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin 63^\\circ}\\)

\n\n

అలాగే, \\(\\sin 63^\\circ = \\cos 27^\\circ\\) (\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\) కాబట్టి).

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ = \\dfrac{1}{\\cos 27^\\circ}\\)

\n\n

తిరిగి ప్రతిక్షేపించండి:

\n\n

\\((1 + 1 - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\times \\dfrac{1}{\\cos 27^\\circ})\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((1 + 1 - 2)\\) = 0

\n\n

∴ సరైన సమాధానం ఎంపిక (1).

गणना:

\n\n

पदावली: \\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\) आणि \\(\\cos (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\sin \\theta\\) वापरून:

\n\n

\\(\\sin 55^\\circ = \\cos 35^\\circ\\) आणि \\(\\cos 79^\\circ = \\sin 11^\\circ\\)

\n\n

पदावली अशी बनते:

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\cos 35^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\sin 11^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((1 + 1 - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ)\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin 63^\\circ}\\)

\n\n

तसेच, \\(\\sin 63^\\circ = \\cos 27^\\circ\\) (जसे \\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\))

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ = \\dfrac{1}{\\cos 27^\\circ}\\)

\n\n

पुन्हा मूल्ये ठेवल्यास:

\n\n

\\((1 + 1 - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\times \\dfrac{1}{\\cos 27^\\circ})\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((1 + 1 - 2)\\) = 0

\n\n

∴ योग्य उत्तर पर्याय (1) आहे.

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin \\theta}\\) ஐப் பயன்படுத்தி:

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin 63^\\circ}\\)

\n\n

மேலும், \\(\\sin 63^\\circ = \\cos 27^\\circ\\) (ஏனெனில் \\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\)).

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ = \\dfrac{1}{\\cos 27^\\circ}\\)

\n\n

திருப்பி பிரதியிடவும்:

\n\n

\\((1 + 1 - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\times \\dfrac{1}{\\cos 27^\\circ})\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\((1 + 1 - 2)\\) = 0

\n\n

∴ சரியான பதில் விருப்பம் (1).

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ଅଭିବ୍ୟକ୍ତି: \\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\)

\n\n

\\(\\cos (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\sin \\theta\\)

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin \\theta}\\)

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

ଅଭିବ୍ୟକ୍ତି: \\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\) ଏବଂ \\(\\cos (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\sin \\theta\\) ବ୍ୟବହାର କରି:

\n\n

\\(\\sin 55^\\circ = \\cos 35^\\circ\\) ଏବଂ \\(\\cos 79^\\circ = \\sin 11^\\circ\\)

\n\n

ଅଭିବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ହେଲା:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ = \\dfrac{1}{\\cos 27^\\circ}\\)

\n\n

ପୁନଃ ସ୍ଥାପନ କରନ୍ତୁ:

\n\n5a:T915,\n

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ:

\n\n

ਵਿਅੰਜਕ: \\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਫਾਰਮੂਲਾ:

\n\n

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ:

\n\n

ਵਿਅੰਜਕ: \\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਫਾਰਮੂਲਾ:

\n\n

\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\)

\n\n

\\(\\cos (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\sin \\theta\\)

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta = \\dfrac{1}{\\sin \\theta}\\)

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾਵਾਂ:

\n\n

ਵਿਅੰਜਕ: \\(\\dfrac{\\cos 35^\\circ}{\\sin 55^\\circ} + \\dfrac{\\sin 11^\\circ}{\\cos 79^\\circ} - 2 \\cos 27^\\circ \\operatorname{cosec} 63^\\circ\\)

\n\n

\\(\\sin (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\cos \\theta\\) ਅਤੇ \\(\\cos (90^\\circ - \\theta) = \\sin \\theta\\) ਦੀ ਵਰਤੋਂ ਕਰਦੇ ਹੋਏ:

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Let the present age of Sujatha be S and Vanita be V.

\n

From the first condition: 6S = 3V + 6 → 2S − V = 2 → V = 2S − 2.

\n

From the second condition, after 7 years: 4(V + 7) = 6(S + 7) − 6 → 4V + 28 = 6S + 36 → 4V − 6S = 8.

\n

Substitute V = 2S − 2 into the second equation: 4(2S − 2) − 6S = 8 → 2S = 16 → S = 8 years, and V = 14 years.

\n

The difference k = V − S = 14 − 8 = 6 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6 years.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Let Sujatha's present age be S and Vanita's present age be V.

\n

According to the first statement:

\n

⇒ 6S = 3V + 6

\n

⇒ 2S = V + 2

\n

⇒ V = 2S − 2   —— (Equation 1)

\n

According to the second statement, after 7 years:

\n

⇒ Age of Sujatha after 7 years = S + 7

\n

⇒ Age of Vanita after 7 years = V + 7

\n

⇒ 4(V + 7) = 6(S + 7) − 6

\n

⇒ 4V + 28 = 6S + 42 − 6

\n

⇒ 4V + 28 = 6S + 36

\n

⇒ 4V − 6S = 8

\n

⇒ 2V − 3S = 4   —— (Equation 2)

\n

Substitute Equation 1 into Equation 2:

\n

⇒ 2(2S − 2) − 3S = 4

\n

⇒ 4S − 4 − 3S = 4

\n

⇒ S = 8

\n

Substitute S = 8 into Equation 1:

\n

⇒ V = 2(8) − 2 = 14

\n

We are given that Vanita is k years older than Sujatha:

\n

⇒ V = S + k

\n

⇒ 14 = 8 + k

\n

⇒ k = 6

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6 years.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Linear Equations in Two Variables

\n

Problems on ages are solved by formulating algebraic equations based on present conditions and past/future shifts, then using substitution or elimination methods.

\n

Age Difference Invariance

\n

The difference in age between two people remains constant throughout their lives. Thus, the value of k will always be the same regardless of the number of years passed. Shortcut Trick\n

माना सुजाता की वर्तमान आयु S और वनिता की वर्तमान आयु V है।

\n

पहली शर्त से: 6S = 3V + 6 → 2S − V = 2 → V = 2S − 2.

\n

दूसरी शर्त से, 7 वर्ष बाद: 4(V + 7) = 6(S + 7) − 6 → 4V + 28 = 6S + 36 → 4V − 6S = 8.

\n

दूसरी समीकरण में V = 2S − 2 रखने पर: 4(2S − 2) − 6S = 8 → 2S = 16 → S = 8 वर्ष, और V = 14 वर्ष।

\n

अंतर k = V − S = 14 − 8 = 6 वर्ष।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 6 वर्ष है।

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

माना सुजाता की वर्तमान आयु S और वनिता की वर्तमान आयु V है।

\n

पहले कथन के अनुसार:

\n

⇒ 6S = 3V + 6

\n

⇒ 2S = V + 2

\n

⇒ V = 2S − 2 —— (समीकरण 1)

\n

दूसरे कथन के अनुसार, 7 वर्ष बाद:

\n

⇒ 7 वर्ष बाद सुजाता की आयु = S + 7

\n

⇒ 7 वर्ष बाद वनिता की आयु = V + 7

\n

⇒ 4(V + 7) = 6(S + 7) − 6

\n

⇒ 4V + 28 = 6S + 42 − 6

\n

⇒ 4V + 28 = 6S + 36

\n

⇒ 4V − 6S = 8

\n

⇒ 2V − 3S = 4 —— (समीकरण 2)

\n

समीकरण 2 में समीकरण 1 रखने पर:

\n

⇒ 2(2S − 2) − 3S = 4

\n

⇒ 4S − 4 − 3S = 4

\n

⇒ S = 8

\n

समीकरण 1 में S = 8 रखने पर:

\n

⇒ V = 2(8) − 2 = 14

\n

हमें दिया गया है कि वनिता, सुजाता से k वर्ष बड़ी है:

\n

⇒ V = S + k

\n

⇒ 14 = 8 + k

\n

⇒ k = 6

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 6 वर्ष है।

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

दो चरों वाले रैखिक समीकरण

\n

आयु संबंधी समस्याओं को वर्तमान स्थितियों और अतीत/भविष्य के परिवर्तनों के आधार पर बीजगणितीय समीकरण बनाकर, फिर प्रतिस्थापन या विलोपन विधियों का उपयोग करके हल किया जाता है।

\n

आयु अंतर की अपरिवर्तनीयता

\n

दो व्यक्तियों के बीच आयु का अंतर उनके पूरे जीवनकाल में स्थिर रहता है। इस प्रकार, बीतने वाले वर्षों की संख्या के बावजूद k का मान हमेशा समान रहेगा।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

∴ సరైన సమాధానం ఎంపిక (2).

ઉપયોગમાં લેવાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

સુજાથાની ઉંમર (S) અને વનિતાની ઉંમર (V) શોધવા માટે રેખીય સમીકરણો:

\n\n

6S = 3V + 6---- (i)

\n\n

4(V + 7) = 6(S + 7) - 6 --- (ii)

\n\n

k = V - S

\n\n

ગણતરી:

\n\n

પ્રથમ સમીકરણમાંથી:

\n\n

6S = 3V + 6

\n\n

⇒ 2S = V + 2

\n\n

⇒ V = 2S - 2

\n\n

બીજા સમીકરણમાં V = 2S - 2 મૂકતા:

\n\n

4((2S - 2) + 7) = 6(S + 7) - 6

\n\n

⇒ 4(2S + 5) = 6S + 42 - 6

\n\n

⇒ 8S + 20 = 6S + 36

\n\n

⇒ 8S - 6S = 36 - 20

\n\n

⇒ 2S = 16

\n\n

⇒ S = 8

\n\n

V શોધો:

\n\n

V = 2S - 2

\n\n

⇒ V = 2(8) - 2

\n\n

⇒ V = 16 - 2

\n\n

⇒ V = 14

\n\n

k શોધો:

\n\n

k = V - S

\n\n

⇒ k = 14 - 8

\n\n

⇒ k = 6

\n\n

∴ સાચો જવાબ વિકલ્પ (2) છે.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ V = 16 - 2

⇒ V = 14

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ k = 6

"end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Given secθ + tanθ = 2. Then secθ − tanθ = 1/2.

\n

Adding both: 2secθ = 2 + 1/2 = 5/2 ⇒ secθ = 5/4.

\n

Find: 3secθ + 4 = 3(5/4) + 4 = 15/4 + 16/4 = 31/4.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 31/4.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given: secθ + tanθ = 2

\n

Formula Used: sec2θ − tan2θ = 1 ⇒ (secθ + tanθ)(secθ − tanθ) = 1

\n

⇒ 2 × (secθ − tanθ) = 1

\n

⇒ secθ − tanθ = 1/2

\n

⇒ (secθ + tanθ) + (secθ − tanθ) = 2 + 1/2

\n

⇒ 2secθ = 5/2

\n

⇒ secθ = 5/4

\n

⇒ 3secθ + 4 = 3 × (5/4) + 4

\n

⇒ 15/4 + 4 = (15 + 16)/4 = 31/4

\n

∴ The correct answer is 31/4.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identity

\n

The identity 1 + tan2θ = sec2θ leads to the reciprocal relationship (secθ + tanθ) = 1 / (secθ − tanθ).

\n

Cosecant Identity

\n

Similarly, 1 + cot2θ = cosec2θ, meaning (cosecθ + cotθ) = 1 / (cosecθ − cotθ).

\n

Pythagorean Triplets

\n

Since secθ = 5/4 (Hypotenuse/Base), the Perpendicular is √(52 − 42) = 3, forming a 3-4-5 triplet. Shortcut Trick\n

Given sin A = 3/4 (Perpendicular/Hypotenuse)

\n

Base = √(42 − 32) = √(16 − 9) = √7

\n

cos A = Base/Hypotenuse = √7/4

\n

cot A = Base/Perpendicular = √7/3

\n

cos A × cot A = (√7/4) × (√7/3) = 7/12

\n

∴ The correct answer is 7/12.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given: sin A = 3/4

\n

Formula Used: sin A = P/H, cos A = B/H, cot A = B/P, B = √(H2 − P2)

\n

⇒ P = 3, H = 4

\n

⇒ B = √(42 − 32) = √(16 − 9) = √7

\n

⇒ cos A = B/H = √7/4

\n

⇒ cot A = B/P = √7/3

\n

⇒ Product = cos A × cot A = (√7/4) × (√7/3)

\n

⇒ Product = 7/12

\n

∴ The correct answer is 7/12.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Fundamental identity: sin2A + cos2A = 1. Therefore, cos A = √(1 − sin2A).

\n

Pythagorean Triplet

\n

For any right-angled triangle, (Perpendicular)2 + (Base)2 = (Hypotenuse)2.

\n

Ratio Relation

\n

cot A can also be expressed as cos A / sin A. Thus, cos A × cot A = cos2A / sin A. Shortcut Trick\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

Put θ = 90° in the given expression.

\n

⇒ 4/sec290° + 3/(1+cot290°) + sin290°

\n

⇒ 4/∞ + 3/(1+0) + (1)2

\n

⇒ 0 + 3 + 1 = 4

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given: Expression = 4/sec2θ + 3/(1+cot2θ) + sin2θ

\n

Formula Used: 1/secθ = cosθ, 1 + cot2θ = cosec2θ, 1/cosecθ = sinθ, sin2θ + cos2θ = 1

\n

⇒ 4 × (1/sec2θ) + 3/(cosec2θ) + sin2θ

\n

⇒ 4cos2θ + 3sin2θ + sin2θ

\n

⇒ 4cos2θ + 4sin2θ

\n

⇒ 4(cos2θ + sin2θ)

\n

⇒ 4 × 1 = 4

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Pythagorean Identities

\n

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

Key ratios: cosθ = 1/secθ, sinθ = 1/cosecθ, and tanθ = 1/cotθ. Shortcut Trick\n

Given: sinB = √3/2 ⇒ B = 60°.

\n

Put B = 60° in √3 cosecB + secB − 4.

\n

⇒ √3 × (2/√3) + 2 − 4

\n

⇒ 2 + 2 − 4 = 0.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: sinB = √3/2

\n

Formula Used: cosecB = 1/sinB; secB = 1/cosB; sin2B + cos2B = 1

\n
\n

⇒ sinB = √3/2 ⇒ Perpendicular(P) = √3, Hypotenuse(H) = 2

\n

⇒ Base(B) = √(H2 − P2) = √(22 − (√3)2) = √(4 − 3) = 1

\n

⇒ cosecB = H/P = 2/√3

\n

⇒ secB = H/B = 2/1 = 2

\n

⇒ Expression = (√3 × 2/√3) + 2 − 4

\n

⇒ Expression = 2 + 2 − 4 = 0

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Table Values (60°)

\n

sin60° = √3/2, cos60° = 1/2, tan60° = √3, cot60° = 1/√3, sec60° = 2, cosec60° = 2/√3.

\n

Reciprocal Identities

\n

sinθ = 1/cosecθ, cosθ = 1/secθ, tanθ = 1/cotθ.

\n

Pythagorean Triplets

\n

For a right-angled triangle, a2 + b2 = c2. Standard triplets include (3, 4, 5), (5, 12, 13), and (1, √3, 2). Shortcut Trick\n

ਜੇ 3 cotA = 2, ਤਾਂ \\(\\sqrt{\\frac{3(\\operatorname{cosec}^2 A - 1)}{(\\sec^2 A - 1)}}\\) ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ।
\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "\\(\\frac{2\\sqrt{3}}{3}\\)"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "\\(\\frac{4\\sqrt{3}}{9}\\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(\\frac{2\\sqrt{3}}{9}\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(\\frac{4\\sqrt{3}}{3}\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Given: 3 cotA = 2 ⇒ cotA = 2/3.

\n

The expression simplifies using trigonometric identities: cosec2A − 1 = cot2A and sec2A − 1 = tan2A.

\n

Value = √[3 × cot2A / tan2A] = √[3 × cot4A] = cot2A × √3.

\n

Value = (2/3)2 × √3 = (4/9) × √3 = 4√3/9.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4√3/9.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: 3 cotA = 2

\n

Formula Used: 1 + cot2A = cosec2A; 1 + tan2A = sec2A; tanA = 1/cotA

\n
\n

⇒ cotA = 2/3

\n

⇒ cosec2A − 1 = cot2A = (2/3)2 = 4/9

\n

⇒ sec2A − 1 = tan2A = (3/2)2 = 9/4

\n

⇒ Expression = √[3 × (4/9) / (9/4)]

\n

⇒ Expression = √[3 × (4/9) × (4/9)]

\n

⇒ Expression = (4/9) × √3 = 4√3/9

\n

∴ The correct answer is 4√3/9.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Fundamental Identities

\n

sin2θ + cos2θ = 1; sec2θ − tan2θ = 1; cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1.

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

tanθ = 1/cotθ; sinθ = 1/cosecθ; cosθ = 1/secθ. Shortcut Trick\n

दिया गया है: 3 cotA = 2 ⇒ cotA = 2/3.

\n

trigonometric identities का उपयोग करके व्यंजक सरल हो जाता है: cosec2A − 1 = cot2A और sec2A − 1 = tan2A.

\n

मान = √[3 × cot2A / tan2A] = √[3 × cot4A] = cot2A × √3.

\n

मान = (2/3)2 × √3 = (4/9) × √3 = 4√3/9.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 4√3/9 है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n

\n

\n

\n

\n

\n

Fundamental Identities (मौलिक सर्वसमिकाएँ)

\n

Reciprocal Relations (व्युत्क्रम संबंध)

\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "3 sinA"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "3"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "9"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": " 9 tanA"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "pa": {"value": "
ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ \\(\\frac{3}{sin ^ 2A secA} \\) x \\(3\\sqrt{tan ^ 2A - Sin ^ 2A}\\)

\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "3 sinA"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "3"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "9"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": " 9 tanA"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Assume A = 45° to simplify the trigonometric expression.

\n

sin2A = 1/2, secA = √2, tan2A = 1.

\n

Expression = [3 ÷ (1/2 × √2)] × 3√(1 − 1/2) = [3 × 2 / √2] × 3√(1/2) = 3√2 × 3/√2 = 9.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 9.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: Expr = (3 / (sin2A secA)) × 3√(tan2A − sin2A)

\n

Formula Used: tan2A − sin2A = tan2A sin2A; secA = 1/cosA

\n

⇒ Simplify the square root term: √(tan2A − sin2A) = √((sin2A/cos2A) − sin2A)

\n

⇒ √(sin2A(1/cos2A − 1)) = √(sin2A(sec2A − 1)) = √(sin2A tan2A) = sinA tanA

\n

⇒ Substitute back into the expression: (3 / (sin2A × (1/cosA))) × 3(sinA tanA)

\n

⇒ (3 cosA / sin2A) × 3 sinA × (sinA / cosA)

\n

⇒ (9 × cosA × sin2A) / (sin2A × cosA) = 9

\n

∴ The correct answer is 9.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Product-Difference Identity

\n

For any angle A, tan2A − sin2A = tan2A × sin2A and similarly cot2A − cos2A = cot2A × cos2A.

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

secA = 1/cosA, cosecA = 1/sinA, and tanA = sinA/cosA.

\n

Pythagorean Identities

\n

Fundamental identities: sin2A + cos2A = 1 and 1 + tan2A = sec2A. Shortcut Trick\n

त्रिकोणमितीय व्यंजक को सरल बनाने के लिए A = 45° मान लें।

\n

sin2A = 1/2, secA = √2, tan2A = 1.

\n

व्यंजक = [3 ÷ (1/2 x √2)] x 3√(1 − 1/2) = [3 x 2 / √2] x 3√(1/2) = 3√2 x 3/√2 = 9.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 9 है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है: व्यंजक = (3 / (sin2A secA)) x 3√(tan2A − sin2A)

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र: tan2A − sin2A = tan2A sin2A; secA = 1/cosA

\n

⇒ वर्गमूल पद को सरल करें: √(tan2A − sin2A) = √((sin2A/cos2A) − sin2A)

\n

⇒ √(sin2A(1/cos2A − 1)) = √(sin2A(sec2A − 1)) = √(sin2A tan2A) = sinA tanA

\n

⇒ व्यंजक में मान वापस रखें: (3 / (sin2A x (1/cosA))) x 3(sinA tanA)

\n

⇒ (3 cosA / sin2A) x 3 sinA x (sinA / cosA)

\n

⇒ (9 x cosA x sin2A) / (sin2A x cosA) = 9

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 9 है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

त्रिकोणमितीय गुणनफल-अंतर सर्वसमिका

\n

किसी भी कोण A के लिए, tan2A − sin2A = tan2A x sin2A और इसी प्रकार cot2A − cos2A = cot2A x cos2A.

\n

व्युत्क्रम संबंध

\n

secA = 1/cosA, cosecA = 1/sinA, और tanA = sinA/cosA.

\n

पायथागॉरियन सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

मूल सर्वसमिकाएँ: sin2A + cos2A = 1 और 1 + tan2A = sec2A.

"2"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Given: √3 tanA = 3 sinA

\n

⇒ √3 (sinA/cosA) = 3 sinA

\n

⇒ cosA = √3/3 = 1/√3

\n

Value required: 2√3 cosecA × tanA = 2√3 (1/sinA) × (sinA/cosA) = 2√3/cosA

\n

⇒ 2√3 ÷ (1/√3) = 2√3 × √3 = 6

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given: √3 tanA = 3 sinA

\n

Formula Used: tanA = sinA/cosA, cosecA = 1/sinA

\n

⇒ √3 (sinA/cosA) = 3 sinA

\n

⇒ 1/cosA = 3/√3

\n

⇒ cosA = √3/3 = 1/√3

\n

⇒ Base (B) = 1, Hypotenuse (H) = √3

\n

⇒ Perpendicular (P) = √(H2 − B2) = √(3 − 1) = √2

\n

⇒ Target expression = 2√3 cosecA × tanA

\n

⇒ Target expression = 2√3 × (H/P) × (P/B) = 2√3 × (H/B)

\n

⇒ Target expression = 2√3 × (√3/1)

\n

⇒ 2 × 3 = 6

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Fundamental relations: tanA = sinA/cosA, cotA = cosA/sinA, and sin2A + cos2A = 1.

\n

Reciprocal Ratios

\n

cosecA = 1/sinA, secA = 1/cosA, and cotA = 1/tanA.

\n

Pythagoras Theorem

\n

In a right-angled triangle, P2 + B2 = H2 where P is perpendicular, B is base, and H is hypotenuse. Shortcut Trick\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

sec A) (1 + sec A)} {(1 - cosecA) (1 + cosecA)}\\) ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ।", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "\\(\\frac {16} {9}\\)"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "\\(\\frac {80} {27}\\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(\\frac {256} {81}\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(\\frac {64} {9}\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Given: 3 tan A = 4 → tan A = 4/3.

\n

Expression: (1 − sec A)(1 + sec A) / (1 − cosec A)(1 + cosec A) = (1 − sec2 A) / (1 − cosec2 A).

\n

Using identities: −tan2 A / −cot2 A = tan2 A × tan2 A = tan4 A.

\n

Calculation: (4/3)4 = 256/81.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 256/81.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given: 3 tan A = 4 ⇒ tan A = 4/3 (Perpendicular/Base)

\n

Formula Used: (a − b)(a + b) = a2 − b2; sec2 A − 1 = tan2 A; cosec2 A − 1 = cot2 A

\n

⇒ Let Perpendicular (P) = 4, Base (B) = 3. Using Pythagoras theorem, Hypotenuse (H) = √(42 + 32) = 5.

\n

⇒ sec A = H/B = 5/3 and cosec A = H/P = 5/4.

\n

⇒ Expression = (1 − sec2 A) / (1 − cosec2 A) = −(sec2 A − 1) / −(cosec2 A − 1) = tan2 A / cot2 A.

\n

⇒ tan2 A / (1/tan2 A) = tan4 A.

\n

⇒ (4/3)4 = 256/81.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 256/81.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Standard Pythagorean identities: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, 1 + tan2θ = sec2θ, and 1 + cot2θ = cosec2θ.

\n

Pythagorean Triplets

\n

Common integer sets for right triangles: (3, 4, 5), (5, 12, 13), (8, 15, 17), (7, 24, 25).

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

tan A = sin A / cos A, cot A = 1 / tan A, sec A = 1 / cos A, and cosec A = 1 / sin A. Shortcut Trick\n

दिया गया है: 3 tan A = 4 → tan A = 4/3.

\n

व्यंजक: (1 − sec A)(1 + sec A) / (1 − cosec A)(1 + cosec A) = (1 − sec2 A) / (1 − cosec2 A).

\n

सर्वसमिकाओं का उपयोग करने पर: −tan2 A / −cot2 A = tan2 A × tan2 A = tan4 A.

\n

गणना: (4/3)4 = 256/81.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 256/81 है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

दिया गया है: 3 tan A = 4 ⇒ tan A = 4/3 (लंब/आधार)

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र: (a − b)(a + b) = a2 − b2; sec2 A − 1 = tan2 A; cosec2 A − 1 = cot2 A

\n

⇒ माना लंब (P) = 4, आधार (B) = 3. पाइथागोरस प्रमेय का उपयोग करने पर, कर्ण (H) = √(42 + 32) = 5.

\n

⇒ sec A = H/B = 5/3 और cosec A = H/P = 5/4.

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = (1 − sec2 A) / (1 − cosec2 A) = −(sec2 A − 1) / −(cosec2 A − 1) = tan2 A / cot2 A.

\n

⇒ tan2 A / (1/tan2 A) = tan4 A.

\n

⇒ (4/3)4 = 256/81.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 256/81 है।

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ଯଦି 3 tan A = 4, ତେବେ \\(\\dfrac{(1 - sec A)(1 + sec A)}{(1 - cosec A)(1 + cosec A)}\\) ର ମୂଲ୍ୟ ଖୋଜ |

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

1. tan2A + 1 = sec2A

\n\n

2. cosec2A = 1 + cot2A

\n\n

3. (1 - x)(1 + x) = 1 - x2

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

3 tan A = 4

\n\n

⇒ tan A = 4/3

\n\n

tan2A + 1 = sec2A ବ୍ୟବହାର କରି:

\n\n

⇒ sec2A = (4/3)2 + 1

\n\n

⇒ sec2A = 16/9 + 9/9

\n\n

⇒ sec2A = 25/9

\n\n

⇒ sec A = √(25/9) = 5/3

\n\n

ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ, cosec2A = 1 + cot2A

\n\n

⇒ cot A = 1/tan A = 3/4ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ ਹੈ:

\n\n

ਜੇਕਰ 3 tan A = 4 ਹੈ, ਤਾਂ \\(\\dfrac{(1 - sec A)(1 + sec A)}{(1 - cosec A)(1 + cosec A)}\\) ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ।

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਸੂਤਰ:

\n\n

1. tan2A + 1 = sec2A

\n\n

2. cosec2A = 1 + cot2A

\n\n

3. (1 - x)(1 + x) = 1 - x2

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾਵਾਂ:

\n\n

3 tan A = 4

\n\n

⇒ tan A = 4/3

\n\n

tan2A + 1 = sec2A ਦੀ ਵਰਤੋਂ ਕਰਦੇ ਹੋਏ:

\n\n

⇒ sec2A = (4/3)2 + 1

\n\n

⇒ sec2A = 16/9 + 9/9

\n\n

⇒ sec2A = 25/9

\n\n

⇒ sec A = √(25/9) = 5/3

\n\n

ਹੁਣ, cosec2A = 1 + cot2A

\n\n

⇒ cot A = 1/tan A = 3/4

\n\n

⇒ cosec2A = 1 + (3/4)2

\n\n

⇒ cosec2A = 1 + 9/16

\n\n

⇒ cosec2A = 16/16 + 9/16

\n\n

⇒ cosec2A = 25/16

\n\n

⇒ cosec A = √(25/16) = 5/4

\n\n

\\(\\dfrac{(1 - sec A)(1 + sec A)}{(1 - cosec A)(1 + cosec A)}\\) ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{1 - sec^2 A}{1 - cosec^2 A}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{1 - (5/3)^2}{1 - (5/4)^2}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{1 - 25/9}{1 - 25/16}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{(9/9 - 25/9)}{(16/16 - 25/16)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{(-16/9)}{(-9/16)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{16}{9} \\times \\dfrac{16}{9}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{256}{81}\\)

\n\n

∴ ਸਹੀ ਉੱਤਰ ਵਿਕਲਪ (3) ਹੈ।

Apply Trigonometric Identities directly: 1 + tan2A = sec2A and 1 + cot2A = cosec2A.

\n

Numerator: 3(1 + tan2A)cotA = 3sec2A cotA = 3 × (1/cos2A) × (cosA/sinA) = 3/(cosA sinA).

\n

Denominator: (1 + cot2A)secA = cosec2A secA = (1/sin2A) × (1/cosA) = 1/(sin2A cosA).

\n

Ratio: [3/(cosA sinA)] ÷ [1/(sin2A cosA)] = [3/(cosA sinA)] × [sin2A cosA] = 3sinA.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3sinA.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: Expression = [(3 + 3tan2A)cotA] / [(1 + cot2A)secA]

\n

Formula Used: 1 + tan2A = sec2A; 1 + cot2A = cosec2A; secA = 1/cosA; cosecA = 1/sinA; cotA = cosA/sinA.

\n

⇒ Expression = [3(1 + tan2A)cotA] / [(1 + cot2A)secA]

\n

⇒ Expression = [3sec2A × cotA] / [cosec2A × secA]

\n

⇒ Expression = [3secA × cotA] / [cosec2A]

\n

⇒ Expression = [3 × (1/cosA) × (cosA/sinA)] / [1/sin2A]

\n

⇒ Expression = [3/sinA] / [1/sin2A]

\n

⇒ Expression = (3/sinA) × sin2A

\n

⇒ Expression = 3sinA

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3sinA.

Pythagorean Identities

\n

Fundamental relations: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, 1 + tan2θ = sec2θ, and 1 + cot2θ = cosec2θ.

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

secθ = 1/cosθ, cosecθ = 1/sinθ, and cotθ = 1/tanθ.

\n

Ratio Identities

\n

tanθ = sinθ/cosθ and cotθ = cosθ/sinθ. Shortcut Trick\n

सीधे Trigonometric Identities का उपयोग करें: 1 + tan2A = sec2A और 1 + cot2A = cosec2A.

\n

अंश (Numerator): 3(1 + tan2A)cotA = 3sec2A cotA = 3 × (1/cos2A) × (cosA/sinA) = 3/(cosA sinA).

\n

हर (Denominator): (1 + cot2A)secA = cosec2A secA = (1/sin2A) × (1/cosA) = 1/(sin2A cosA).

\n

अनुपात: [3/(cosA sinA)] ÷ [1/(sin2A cosA)] = [3/(cosA sinA)] × [sin2A cosA] = 3sinA.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 3sinA है।

\n

\n

दिया गया है: व्यंजक = [(3 + 3tan2A)cotA] / [(1 + cot2A)secA]

\n

\n

\n

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = [3secA × cotA] / [cosec2A]

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = [3 × (1/cosA) × (cosA/sinA)] / [1/sin2A]

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = [3/sinA] / [1/sin2A]

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = (3/sinA) × sin2A

\n

⇒ व्यंजक = 3sinA

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:", "or": "

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

Expression = (3 + 3 tan2A) cot A ÷ (1 + cot2A) sec A

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

1 + tan2A = sec2A

\n\n

1 + cot2A = csc2A

\n\n

cot A = cos A &di\nvide; sin A, sec A = 1 ÷ cos A

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

⇒ 3 + 3 tan2A = 3(1 + tan2A) = 3 sec2A

\n\n

⇒ ଡେନୋମିନେଟର = (1 + cot2A) sec A = csc2A x sec A

\n\n

⇒ Expression = [3 sec2A x cot A] ÷ [csc2A x sec A]

\n\n

⇒ = 3 x (sec2A ÷ sec A) x (cot A ÷ csc2A)

\n\n

⇒ = 3 sec A x [ (cos A ÷ sin A) ÷ (1 ÷ sin2A) ]

\n\n

⇒ = 3 sec A x (cos A ÷ sin A) x sin2A

\n\n

⇒ = 3 x (1 ÷ cos A) x (cos A ÷ sin A) x sin2A

\n\n

⇒ = 3 x (1 ÷ sin A) x sin2A

\n\n

⇒ = 3 sin A

\n\n

∴ Expression ର ମୂଲ୍ୟ = 3 sin A

7secA(1 + sin A) (sec A − tanA) − 4ର ମୂଲ୍ୟ ଖୋଜ ।

", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "10"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "7"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "4"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "3"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "pa": {"value": "7secA(1 + sin A) (sec A − tanA) − 4 ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਪਤਾ ਕਰੋ।", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "10"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "7"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "4"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "3"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Put A = 0° as the expression is valid for all values of A.

\n

sin 0° = 0, cos 0° = 1, sec 0° = 1, tan 0° = 0.

\n

⇒ 7 × sec 0° (1 + sin 0°) (sec 0° − tan 0°) − 4

\n

⇒ 7 × 1 (1 + 0) (1 − 0) − 4 = 7 × 1 × 1 × 1 − 4 = 7 − 4 = 3.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: 7sec A (1 + sin A) (sec A − tan A) − 4

\n

Formula Used: sec A = 1/cos A, tan A = sin A/cos A, (a + b)(a − b) = a2 − b2, 1 − sin2A = cos2A

\n

⇒ 7sec A (1 + sin A) (1/cos A − sin A/cos A) − 4

\n

⇒ 7sec A (1 + sin A) [(1 − sin A) / cos A] − 4

\n

⇒ 7sec A [(1 + sin A)(1 − sin A) / cos A] − 4

\n

⇒ 7sec A [(1 − sin2A) / cos A] − 4

\n

⇒ 7(1/cos A) [cos2A / cos A] − 4

\n

⇒ 7(1/cos A) × cos A − 4

\n

⇒ 7 − 4 = 3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

sin2A + cos2A = 1, sec2A − tan2A = 1, and cosec2A − cot2A = 1.

\n

Value Substitution Method

\n

In trigonometric expressions with no conditions, substituting standard angles like 0°, 30°, 45° often yields the result quickly. Shortcut Trick\n

A = 0° रखें क्योंकि यह व्यंजक A के सभी मानों के लिए मान्य है।

\n

sin 0° = 0, cos 0° = 1, sec 0° = 1, tan 0° = 0.

\n

⇒ 7 × sec 0° (1 + sin 0°) (sec 0° − tan 0°) − 4

\n

⇒ 7 × 1 (1 + 0) (1 − 0) − 4 = 7 × 1 × 1 × 1 − 4 = 7 − 4 = 3.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 3 है।

\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ \\(7 - 4 = 3\\)

நாம் முதலில் \\(7\\sec A(1 + \\sin A)(\\sec A - \\tan A)\\) என்ற பகுதியைக் குறைப்போம்.

\n\n

⇒ \\(7 \\times \\frac{1}{\\cos A} (1 + \\sin A) (\\frac{1}{\\cos A} - \\frac{\\sin A}{\\cos A})\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(7 \\times \\frac{1}{\\cos A} (1 + \\sin A) \\left(\\frac{1 - \\sin A}{\\cos A}\\right)\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{7(1 + \\sin A)(1 - \\sin A)}{\\cos A \\cdot \\cos A}\\)

\n\n

⇒ இயற்கணித முற்றொருமை \\((a+b)(a-b) = a^2 - b^2\\) ஐ பயன்படுத்தி:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{7(1 - \\sin^2 A)}{\\cos^2 A}\\)

\n\n

⇒ பிதாகரஸ் முற்றொருமை \\(1 - \\sin^2 A = \\cos^2 A\\) ஐ பயன்படுத்தி:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{7(\\cos^2 A)}{\\cos^2 A}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(7\\)

\n\n

இப்போது, இதை அசல் கோவையில் மீண்டும் பிரதியிடுவோம்:

\n\n

⇒ \\(7 - 4 = 3\\)

\n\n

∴ கோவையின் மதிப்பு 3 ஆகும்.

\n\n9a:T84b,\n

Put A = 45° in the expression.

\n

⇒ [2(1/√2)/(1 − 1/√2) + 2(1/√2)/(1 + 1/√2)] × 3√((√2)2 − 1)

\n

⇒ [√2/((√2−1)/√2) + √2/((√2+1)/√2)] × 3(1) ⇒ [2/(√2−1) + 2/(√2+1)] × 3

\n

\n

Checking options: (2) 12 sec45°tan45° = 12 × √2 × 1 = 12√2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 12 secAtanA.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: [2cosA/(1 − sinA) + 2cosA/(1 + sinA)] × 3√(sec2A − 1)

\n

Formula Used: (1 − sin2A) = cos2A and (sec2A − 1) = tan2A

\n

\n

\n

⇒ [ 4 / cosA ] × 3tanA

\n

⇒ 4secA × 3tanA

\n

⇒ 12secAtanA

\n

∴ The correct answer is 12 secAtanA.

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

secA = 1/cosA, cosecA = 1/sinA, and tanA = sinA/cosA.

\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "6"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "4"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "10"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "9"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Let V be Vashnavi age and S be Sooraj age.

\n

From given: 5V − 7S = 18 and 5V − 8S = 17.

\n

Subtracting the equations: (5V − 7S) − (5V − 8S) = 18 − 17 ⇒ S = 1.

\n

Substitute S = 1 in 5V − 8S = 17 ⇒ 5V − 8 = 17 ⇒ 5V = 25 ⇒ V = 5.

\n

Sum of ages = 5 + 1 = 6 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6 years.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

5 × Vashnavi age = 7 × Sooraj age + 18

\n

8 × Sooraj age = 5 × Vashnavi age − 17

\n
\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let Vashnavi age be V and Sooraj age be S.

\n

⇒ 5V = 7S + 18 --- (i)

\n

⇒ 8S = 5V − 17 --- (ii)

\n

⇒ Substitute 5V from (i) into (ii):

\n

⇒ 8S = (7S + 18) − 17

\n

⇒ 8S = 7S + 1

\n

⇒ S = 1 year

\n

⇒ Put S = 1 in (i): 5V = 7(1) + 18

\n

⇒ 5V = 25 ⇒ V = 5 years

\n

⇒ Sum = V + S = 5 + 1 = 6 years.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6 years.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Linear Equations in Two Variables

\n

Problems involving ages of two people usually result in a system of two linear equations which can be solved by substitution or elimination.

\n

Age Difference Property

\n

The difference between the ages of two people remains constant regardless of the number of years passed or preceding.

\n

Ratio Method

\n

If the ratio of ages is given at different time intervals, the cross-multiplication method is the fastest way to find individual ages. Shortcut Trick\n

माना V वैष्णवी की आयु है और S सूरज की आयु है।

\n

दिया गया है: 5V − 7S = 18 और 5V − 8S = 17.

\n

समीकरणों को घटाने पर: (5V − 7S) − (5V − 8S) = 18 − 17 ⇒ S = 1.

\n

S = 1 को 5V − 8S = 17 में रखने पर ⇒ 5V − 8 = 17 ⇒ 5V = 25 ⇒ V = 5.

\n

आयु का योग = 5 + 1 = 6 वर्ष।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 6 वर्ष है।

\n

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है:

\n

5 × वैष्णवी की आयु = 7 × सूरज की आयु + 18

\n

8 × सूरज की आयु = 5 × वैष्णवी की आयु − 17

\n
\n

गणना:

\n

⇒ माना वैष्णवी की आयु V है और सूरज की आयु S है।

\n

⇒ 5V = 7S + 18 --- (i)

\n

⇒ 8S = 5V − 17 --- (ii)

\n

⇒ (i) से 5V का मान (ii) में रखने पर:

\n

⇒ 8S = (7S + 18) − 17

\n

⇒ 8S = 7S + 1

\n

⇒ S = 1 वर्ष

\n

⇒ S = 1 को (i) में रखने पर: 5V = 7(1) + 18

\n

⇒ 5V = 25 ⇒ V = 5 वर्ष

\n

⇒ योग = V + S = 5 + 1 = 6 वर्ष।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 6 वर्ष है।

\n

Additional Information

\n

दो चरों वाले रैखिक समीकरण

\n

दो लोगों की आयु से संबंधित समस्याओं के परिणामस्वरूप आमतौर पर दो रैखिक समीकरणों का एक निकाय बनता है जिसे प्रतिस्थापन या विलोपन द्वारा हल किया जा सकता है।

\n

आयु अंतर गुण

\n

दो व्यक्तियों की आयु के बीच का अंतर बीते हुए या आने वाले वर्षों की संख्या के बावजूद स्थिर रहता है।

\n

अनुपात विधि

\n

यदि विभिन्न समय अंतरालों पर आयु का अनुपात दिया गया है, तो व्यक्तिगत आयु ज्ञात करने के लिए वज्र-गुणन (cross-multiplication) विधि सबसे तेज़ तरीका है।

\nd5:T748,

ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

వైష్ణవి యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు ఐదు రెట్లు, సూరజ్ యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు ఏడు రెట్ల కంటే 18 సంవత్సరాలు ఎక్కువ.

\n\n

సూరజ్ యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు ఎనిమిది రెట్లు, వైష్ణవి యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు ఐదు రెట్ల కంటే 17 సంవత్సరాలు తక్కువ.

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

వైష్ణవి యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = V అనుకుందాం

\n\n

సూరజ్ యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = S అనుకుందాం

\n\n

లెక్కింపు:

\n\n

సమీకరణం 1: 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

సమీకరణం 2: 8S = 5V - 17

\n\n

సమీకరణం 1 నుండి:

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

⇒ V = (7S + 18) / 5

\n\n

V = (7S + 18) / 5 ను సమీకరణం 2 లో ప్రతిక్షేపించండి:

\n\n

⇒ 8S = 5((7S + 18) / 5) - 17

\n\n

⇒ 8S = (7S + 18) - 17

\n\n

⇒ S = 1

\n\n

S = 1 ను సమీకరణం 1 లో ప్రతిక్షేపించండి:

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7(1) + 18

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7 + 18

\n\n

⇒ V = 5

\n\n

వారి వయస్సుల మొత్తం = V + S = 5 + 1 = 6

\n\n

∴ సరైన సమాధానం ఎంపిక (1).

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

వైష్ణవి యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = V అనుకుందాం

\n\n

సూరజ్ యొక్క ప్రస్తుత వయస్సు = S అనుకుందాం

\n\n

లెక్కింపు:

\n\n

సమీకరణం 1: 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

సమీకరణం 2: 8S = 5V - 17

\n\n

సమీకరణం 1 నుండి:

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

⇒ V = (7S + 18) / 5

\n\n

V = (7S + 18) / 5 ను సమీకరణం 2 లో ప్రతిక్షేపించండి:दिलेले आहे:

\n\n

वैष्णवीच्या सध्याच्या वयाची पाच पट ही सूरजच्या सध्याच्या वयाच्या सात पटीपेक्षा 18 वर्षांनी जास्त आहे.

\n\n

सूरजच्या सध्याच्या वयाची आठ पट ही वैष्णवीच्या सध्याच्या वयाच्या पाच पटीपेक्षा 17 वर्षांनी कमी आहे.

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

समजा, वैष्णवीचे सध्याचे वय = V

\n\n

समजा, सूरजचे सध्याचे वय = S

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

समीकरण 1: 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

समीकरण 2: 8S = 5V - 17

\n\n

समीकरण 1 वरून:

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

⇒ V = (7S + 18) / 5

\n\n

समीकरण 2 मध्ये, V = (7S + 18) / 5 ठेवू:

\n\n

⇒ 8S = 5((7S + 18) / 5) - 17

\n\n

⇒ 8S = (7S + 18) - 17

\n\n

⇒ S = 1

\n\n

समीकरण 1 मध्ये, S = 1 ठेवू:

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7(1) + 18

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7 + 18

\n\n

⇒ V = 5

\n\n

त्यांच्या वयाची बेरीज = V + S = 5 + 1 = 6

\n\n

∴ योग्य उत्तर पर्याय (1) आहे.

ଭାସ୍ନାଭୀଙ୍କର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ଗୁଣ, ସୂରଜଙ୍କର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସର ସାତ ଗୁଣଠାରୁ 18 ବର୍ଷ ଅଧିକ ଅଟେ।

\n\n

ସୂରଜଙ୍କର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସର ଆଠ ଗୁଣ, ଭାସ୍ନାଭୀଙ୍କର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସର ପାଞ୍ଚ ଗୁଣଠାରୁ 17 ବର୍ଷ କମ୍ ଅଟେ।

\n\n

ସୂତ୍ର ବ୍ୟବହାର କରାଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ଧରାଯାଉ ଭାସ୍ନାଭୀଙ୍କର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସ = V

\n\n

ଧରାଯାଉ ସୂରଜଙ୍କର ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନର ବୟସ = S

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

ସମୀକରଣ 1: 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

ସମୀକରଣ 2: 8S = 5V - 17

\n\n

ସମୀକରଣ 1 ରୁ:

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7S + 18

\n\n

⇒ V = (7S + 18) / 5

\n\n

ସମୀକରଣ 2 ରେ V = (7S + 18) / 5 ବସାନ୍ତୁ:

\n\n

⇒ 8S = 5((7S + 18) / 5) - 17

\n\n

⇒ 8S = (7S + 18) - 17

\n\n

⇒ S = 1

\n\n

ସମୀକରଣ 1 ରେ S = 1 ବସାନ୍ତୁ:

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7(1) + 18

\n\n

⇒ 5V = 7 + 18

\n\n

⇒ V = 5

\n\n

ସେମାନଙ୍କର ବୟସର ସମଷ୍ଟି = V + S = 5 + 1 = 6

\n\n

∴ ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତର ହେଉଛି ବିକଳ୍ପ (1)।

Convert the given equation to tan A: √2 ÷ sin A = 2 ÷ cos A

\n

⇒ cos A ÷ sin A = 2 ÷ √2

\n

⇒ cot A = √2 ⇒ tan A = 1/√2

\n

Using sec2A = 1 + tan2A ⇒ sec A = √(1 + 1/2) = √(3/2)

\n

⇒ tan A × sec A = (1/√2) × (√3/√2) = √3/2

\n

∴ The correct answer is √3/2.

\n

Alternate Method

\n
\n

Given: √2 cosec A = 2 sec A

\n

Formula: cosec A = 1/sin A, sec A = 1/cos A, tan A = sin A/cos A

\n

⇒ √2 / sin A = 2 / cos A

\n

⇒ sin A / cos A = √2 / 2

\n

⇒ tan A = 1 / √2

\n

⇒ Let Perpendicular (P) = 1 and Base (B) = √2

\n

⇒ Hypotenuse (H) = √(P2 + B2) = √(12 + (√2)2) = √3

\n

⇒ sec A = H / B = √3 / √2

\n

⇒ tan A × sec A = (1 / √2) × (√3 / √2)

\n

⇒ tan A × sec A = √3 / 2

\n

∴ The correct answer is √3/2.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Fundamental relations: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, 1 + tan2θ = sec2θ, and 1 + cot2θ = cosec2θ.

\n

Ratio Definitions

\n

tan θ = P/B, sec θ = H/B, and cosec θ = H/P where P, B, H are parts of a right-angled triangle. Shortcut Trick\n

ਗਣਨਾਵਾਂ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\dfrac{\\sqrt{2}}{\\sin A}=\\dfrac{2}{\\cos A}\\) (ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\sqrt{2}\\cos A=2\\sin A\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\tan A=\\dfrac{\\sin A}{\\cos A}=\\dfrac{\\sqrt{2}}{2}=\\dfrac{1}{\\sqrt{2}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\( \\sec^2 A=1+\\tan^2 A=1+\\dfrac{1}{2}=\\dfrac{3}{2}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\( \\sec A=\\sqrt{\\dfrac{3}{2}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\( \\tan A \\times \\sec A=\\dfrac{1}{\\sqrt{2}}\\times\\sqrt{\\dfrac{3}{2}}=\\dfrac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\)

\n\n

∴ tanA x secA = \\( \\dfrac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\)

ਸਰਲ ਕਰੋ:

\n", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "\\(2sin\\theta\\)"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "\\(2sec\\theta\\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(2cosec\\theta\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(2cos\\theta\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n Shortcut Trick\n

Put θ = 30° in the given expression.

\n

cot 30° = √3 and cosec 30° = 2.

\n

Expression = [√3 / (2 + 1)] + [(2 + 1) / √3] = [√3 / 3] + [3 / √3] = [1 / √3] + √3 = (1 + 3) / √3 = 4 / √3.

\n

Check options for θ = 30°: 2sec 30° = 2 × (2 / √3) = 4 / √3.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2secθ.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: [cot θ / (cosec θ + 1)] + [(cosec θ + 1) / cot θ]

\n

Formula Used: cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1 ⇒ cot2θ = cosec2θ − 1 = (cosec θ + 1)(cosec θ − 1)

\n

⇒ Taking LCM: [cot2θ + (cosec θ + 1)2] / [cot θ(cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ [cot2θ + cosec2θ + 1 + 2cosec θ] / [cot θ(cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ [(cosec2θ − 1) + cosec2θ + 1 + 2cosec θ] / [cot θ(cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ [2cosec2θ + 2cosec θ] / [cot θ(cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ [2cosec θ (cosec θ + 1)] / [cot θ (cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ 2cosec θ / cot θ = 2(1 / sin θ) / (cos θ / sin θ)

\n

⇒ 2 / cos θ = 2sec θ

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2secθ.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Standard Pythagorean identities include sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, 1 + tan2θ = sec2θ, and 1 + cot2θ = cosec2θ.

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

sec θ = 1/cos θ, cosec θ = 1/sin θ, and cot θ = cos θ/sin θ. Shortcut Trick\n

\n

cot 30° = √3 और cosec 30° = 2.

\n

व्यंजक = [√3 / (2 + 1)] + [(2 + 1) / √3] = [√3 / 3] + [3 / √3] = [1 / √3] + √3 = (1 + 3) / √3 = 4 / √3.

\n

θ = 30° के लिए विकल्पों की जाँच करें: 2sec 30° = 2 × (2 / √3) = 4 / √3.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 2secθ है।

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है: [cot θ / (cosec θ + 1)] + [(cosec θ + 1) / cot θ]

\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र: cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1 ⇒ cot2θ = cosec2θ − 1 = (cosec θ + 1)(cosec θ − 1)

\n

⇒ LCM लेने पर: [cot2θ + (cosec θ + 1)2] / [cot θ(cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ [cot2θ + cosec2θ + 1 + 2cosec θ] / [cot θ(cosec θ + 1)]

\n

\n

⇒ [2cosec2θ + 2cosec θ] / [cot θ(cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ [2cosec θ (cosec θ + 1)] / [cot θ (cosec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ 2cosec θ / cot θ = 2(1 / sin θ) / (cos θ / sin θ)

\n

⇒ 2 / cos θ = 2sec θ

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 2secθ है।

त्रिकोणमितीय सर्वसमिकाएँ

sec θ = 1/cos θ, cosec θ = 1/sin θ, और cot θ = cos θ/sin θ.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

రెండు పదాలను సూక్ష్మీకరించండి:

\n\n

\n\n

రెండవ పదం: \\(\\frac{\\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}} = \\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

రెండు పదాలను కలపండి:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{\\cos θ}{1 + \\sin θ} + \\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2 + 2\\sin θ}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

లవమును కారకంగా విభజించండి:

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ 2 sec θ

cot θ = cos θ / sin θ மற்றும் cosec θ = 1 / sin θ என்பதைப் பிரதியிடவும்:

\n\n

⇒ \\( \\frac{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{1}{\\sin θ} + 1} + \\frac{\\frac{1}{\\sin θ} + 1}{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}\\)

\n\n

இரண்டு சொற்களையும் சுருக்கவும்:

\n\n

முதல் பதம்: \\(\\frac{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\sin θ}} = \\frac{\\cos θ}{1 + \\sin θ}\\)

\n\n

இரண்டாம் பதம்: \\(\\frac{\\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}} = \\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

இரண்டு சொற்களையும் இணைக்கவும்:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{\\cos θ}{1 + \\sin θ} + \\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

மீ.சி.ம (LCM) எடுக்கவும்:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{\\cos2 θ + (1 + \\sin θ)2}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ} \\)

\n\n

(1 + sin θ)2 என்பதை விரிவுபடுத்தவும்:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{\\cos2 θ + 1 + 2\\sin θ + \\sin2 θ}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1 என்ற முக்கோணவியல் முற்றொருமையை பயன்படுத்தவும்:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{1 + 1 + 2\\sin θ}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2 + 2\\sin θ}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

பகுதி எண்ணை காரணிப்படுத்தவும்:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2(1 + \\sin θ)}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

(1 + sin θ) ஐ நீக்கவும்:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

⇒ 2 sec θ

\n\n

சுருக்கப்பட்ட மதிப்பு 2 sec θ ஆகும்.

\n\n

ବ୍ୟବହୃତ ସୂତ୍ର:

\n\n

cot θ = cos θ / sin θ

\n\n

cosec θ = 1 / sin θ

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\cot \\theta}{\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta+1}+\\frac{\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta+1}{\\cot \\theta}\\)

\n\n

cot θ = cos θ / sin θ ଏବଂ cosec θ = 1 / sin θ ବଦଳ କରନ୍ତୁ :

\n\n

⇒ \\( \\frac{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{1}{\\sin θ} + 1} + \\frac{\\frac{1}{\\sin θ} + 1}{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}\\)

\n\n

ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ପଦ: \\(\\frac{\\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}} ", "as": "", "ks": "", "kok": "", "mni": "", "ne": "", "pa": "\n

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ ਹੈ:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\cot \\theta}{\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta+1}+\\frac{\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta+1}{\\cot \\theta}\\)

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਫਾਰਮੂਲਾ:

\n\n

cot θ = cos θ / sin θ

\n\n

cosec θ = 1 / sin θ

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\cot \\theta}{\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta+1}+\\frac{\\operatorname{cosec} \\theta+1}{\\cot \\theta}\\)

\n\n

cot θ = cos θ / sin θ ਅਤੇ cosec θ = 1 / sin θ ਦਾ ਮੁੱਲ ਭਰੋ:

\n\n

⇒ \\( \\frac{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{1}{\\sin θ} + 1} + \\frac{\\frac{1}{\\sin θ} + 1}{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}\\)

\n\n

ਦੋਵਾਂ ਪਦਾਂ ਨੂੰ ਸਰਲ ਕਰੋ:

\n\n

ਪਹਿਲਾ ਪਦ: \\(\\frac{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\sin θ}} = \\frac{\\cos θ}{1 + \\sin θ}\\)

\n\n

ਦੂਜਾ ਪਦ: \\(\\frac{\\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\sin θ}}{\\frac{\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}} = \\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

ਦੋਵਾਂ ਪਦਾਂ ਨੂੰ ਜੋੜੋ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{\\cos θ}{1 + \\sin θ} + \\frac{1 + \\sin θ}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

ਲ.ਸ.ਵ. ਲਓ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{\\cos2 θ + (1 + \\sin θ)2}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ} \\)

\n\n

(1 + sin θ)2 ਦਾ ਵਿਸਤਾਰ ਕਰੋ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{\\cos2 θ + 1 + 2\\sin θ + \\sin2 θ}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1 ਪਛਾਣ ਦੀ ਵਰਤੋਂ ਕਰੋ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{1 + 1 + 2\\sin θ}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2 + 2\\sin θ}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

ਅੰਸ਼ ਦਾ ਗੁਣਨਖੰਡਨ ਕਰੋ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2(1 + \\sin θ)}{(1 + \\sin θ) \\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

(1 + sin θ) ਨੂੰ ਰੱਦ ਕਰੋ:

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{2}{\\cos θ}\\)

\n\n

⇒ 2 sec θ

\n\n

ਸਰਲ ਕੀਤਾ ਗਿਆ ਮੁੱਲ 2 sec θ ਹੈ।
